### Selected Harp UI revisions — 2026-10-18

- Updated only Harp presentation in `components/world-5/hole/part4Caverns.tsx` and the current UI plan. Saved active-string slot numbers sit above native string glyphs. Power and its summary metrics share one desktop row and wrap at narrow widths.
- Scope correction: the prior revision accidentally added numbers to Notes cells. Restored the accepted compact 5×2 Notes grid: each cell places its native icon beside its formatted balance, with no note indices. Numbering remains only on active string slots. All other Harp behavior stays unchanged.
- The complete next-note cost field, including currency icon and separator, is absent at maximum note. At this earlier revision, Opal chance was unavailable; the baseline section below supersedes that display. v1.23 source defines `B = 1 + (B_UPG(39,15) + B_UPG(37,20) × GenINFO[128] + B_UPG(38,30) × GenINFO[129]) / 100` and `min(1, B × (1 − (1 − 0.2^(Holes[7][5]+1))^(max(1, GenINFO[112]/100)))` (`idleon1.23.txt:241038–45,241065–75`; UI percent is `100 × result`, `261983–96`). Domino Effect adds 20 percentage points per string plucked during the current strum; its transient activation counter 128 is absent from the account save/API. Distinct assignment count 129 can be derived from a complete saved layout, but it does not supply the missing activation count. The exact current chance cannot be recovered without assuming a cycle state or another unavailable input. The tooltip explains this limit; no assumed count, estimate, nullable backend helper, or runtime integration was added.
- Removed unsupported active-yield and active-amount diagnostics from string cards. Cards retain known active-effect context, known String E no-trigger state, permanent passive bonuses, saved level/EXP and next-level details. String B is described as reserved; no unsupported diagnostic remains.

#### Harp Opal chance input limit — v1.23 source

- The source formula uses `Holes[7][5]` Harp Opals and saved power through `GenINFO[112]`, plus Stringy Effect's +15 percentage points, Domino Effect's +20 points per string activation (`GenINFO[128]`), and Variety Effect's +30 points per distinct string (`GenINFO[129]`) (`idleon1.23.txt:241038–45,241065–75,261983–96`; upgrade records `HoleBuildingRepo.ts:379–407`).
- `GenINFO[128]` counts newly activated strings and resets at a power-cycle boundary (`idleon1.23.txt:268037–108`). The parser does not import cycle phase history (`hole.tsx:1566–1597`), and the prepared API exposes no current activation count (`1600–1617`). The account snapshot therefore cannot yield exact current chance when Domino Effect is owned without an unsupported cycle assumption. `GenINFO[129]` counts distinct raw saved assignment values and is derivable from a complete saved layout (`idleon1.23.txt:268332–40`); it is not the missing activation counter.
- Source review is bounded and does not establish runtime parity or prove absence of all alias/save-restore paths. No exact instantaneous chance calculation was added; the subsequently approved zero-plucked baseline is implemented as recorded below.

#### Harp Opal competitor research correction — 2026-10-18

- Research in [Toolbox's Harp parser](../../reference-repos/IdleonToolbox/parsers/world-5/caverns/the-harp.ts) found Toolbox computes one saved-state chance using first 11 active layout slots, distinct values including zero, Opals `[7][5]`, saved power `[11][22]`, owned schematic factors, and zero Domino activation count by default (`reference-repos/IdleonToolbox/parsers/world-5/caverns/the-harp.ts:14–24,142–161`; parser mapping `parsers/world-5/hole.ts:72–109`).
- **Correction:** Exact arbitrary-instant strum chance remains unknown without transient progress. But a deterministic saved-state baseline is calculable using zero activated strings, matching Toolbox's explicit default—not by assuming completed strum. The configured save yields `0.8541193272644844%` under that convention. This is isolated-expression evidence, not competitor module/runtime/game parity.
- **Approval recorded — 2026-10-18:** The technical supervisor approved the current plan under delegated authority. Separately, the human approved the zero-activation saved-state baseline product choice (“okedoit”); do not attribute technical plan approval to the human. Next: assign a fresh tests-stage session. Before any domain edit, establish and accept paired applicable v1.23 tests using a real save and game outputs: a core Harp Opal chance comparison with `GenINFO[128]` controlled to zero, plus direct Schematic 39/38 parameter comparisons. All direct parameters must pass normally; use the canonical offline-first/version/save-pairing policy, and do not infer game parity from the isolated expression or candidate fixture’s unproven version.

#### Harp baseline Opal chance backend — 2026-10-18

- Added `Harp.baselineOpalChance`, calculated during Harp preparation from saved Opals and power plus initialized Schematic 39/38 APIs. Stringy Effect contributes its owned 15 points. Owned Variety Effect contributes 30 points per distinct raw value in the active layout prefix, including zero; incomplete required layout leaves the result `null`. Known-unowned Variety needs no layout count. Missing required saved inputs or ownership also leave the result `null`. Domino Effect contributes no baseline term because zero strings have been plucked; this does not expose runtime strum state.
- Formula follows the approved v1.23 source operation order and caps at 1. The retained paired test uses one normal v1.23 comparison: game-controlled zero activation and the six-distinct saved prefix yield `0.008541193272644843` (0.8541193272644844%). This is a scoped baseline comparison, not whole-game parity.
- **Runtime and tests:** `yarn test tests/domains/hole/harp-opal-baseline.test.ts tests/domains/hole/harp-opal-baseline-parameters.test.ts` passed both suites, 4/4 tests. The tests load the retained real save through `loadGameDataFromSave` / `updateIdleonData`, then assert public `hole.harp.baselineOpalChance` against the paired controlled v1.23 result. This exercises normal account init, parse, calculate, and public-result flow; output was `0.008541193272644843`.
- The first run failed because `getExtractedValue` expects a number but received the structured restored-counter object. Replaced that object lookup with `captured`, preserving the same counter-restoration assertion; the rerun passed.
- **Limits:** One paired save, known-owned Stringy and Variety, complete 11-slot prefix, and zero-activation controlled game output. Unknown/missing-input branches, known-unowned Variety, alternate saves, arbitrary-strum parity, full-game parity, UI/browser, suite, build, lint, and typecheck were not checked. No save changed. The delegated reviewer passed with no findings. The user accepted this backend result and its check limits; commit the scoped backend and handoff together. No publication.

#### Fresh independent Harp UI review — 2026-10-18

Reviewed issue #444, the selected part-4 UI plan, current scoped diff, CODING_STANDARDS.md, supplied Harp reference, and current implementation captures/result. Unrelated changes were excluded. Code stayed read-only; no checks were rerun.

**Finding for human decision**

- **Redundant and misleading trigger-status copy — `components/world-5/hole/part4Caverns.tsx:398–411`:** Each string card repeats “Cycle outcome unavailable” beside a second unavailable-effect message. This uses internal terminology without player context; on String E, it contradicts the known “No trigger effect” result. Show known static effect context and only the specific unavailable number where useful. This conflicts with LANG-01 (short, useful player-facing language; avoid jargon and fluff).
- **Impossible note-currency fallback — `components/world-5/hole/part4Caverns.tsx:376–381,395–426`:** When the saved note index is 0–8, the next currency index is 11–19, and all ten note records are present in `HoleCurrencyRepo`. The lookup's optional chaining and “Unavailable” fallback cannot cover a valid save; they instead conceal a broken static-data invariant. Use required access per DATA-01.

The Harp layout matches the approved inner-panel outcomes: plain saved power and rate/cost/Opal summary, native 5×2 notes, saved tune, active slots and ordered strip, six released-string cards with passive and saved EXP detail, and shared Harp Study. The actual capture shows configured-save values and all listed sections; no scope expansion, new calculation, or excessive missing-state guard was found. The source-backed distinction between transient active numbers and permanent passives is retained.

The supplied image is a reference, not a rendered planning preview; the historical preview was withdrawn. The supplied implementation-route capture and runner result are the available rendered evidence. They cover one configured save at 1440×1000 and one EXP tooltip; no game parity, alternate save, unknown-state branch, or longer smoke is claimed. The recorded focused run passed before this review; no browser or other checks ran now.

No code changed, acceptance, commit, or publication occurred.

### Selected Bell separator and Well reference-table revision — 2026-10-18

- Changed only `components/world-5/hole/part4Caverns.tsx` plus the minimum `Well` calculation in `data/domain/world-5/hole/well.tsx`. Bell Overview puts right-edge separators between its visible metric groups; completed-method saves show Opals without an orphaned border. Bell content stays otherwise unchanged.
- Rebuilt only Well's inner section. Expansion summary sums the ten current `WellSediment.expansions` values from `Holes[8][0..9]`; missing levels produce Unavailable. It does not use `Holes[7][0]`, the Well cavern Opal-source counter, or `Holes[11][9]`, the manual Opal-purchase level. The obsolete `Well.expansionCount` parse field was removed. On the configured save, total bar expansions displayed 642.
- **Currencies and earnings:** `Holes[9][0]` is Gravel stock, confirmed by `SedimentRepo` index 0 and `HoleCurrencyRepo` index 0; it is not Opal balance. `CanWeAfford_Well` compares this Gravel stock to the cost derived from `Holes[11][9]`; the purchase spends Gravel and separately calls `createOpal` (`idleon1.23.txt:240780–240794,263580–263587`). The UI shows Gravel balance / cost, using `well.opalAffordable`; the configured save showed `7.89Q / 34.5Q`. Well Opals found use the existing `cavernOpalSources` entry “Purchased an Opal with Well sediment.” `hole.tsx:2282–2306` maps saved `[7][study.index]` to each cavern source; Well index 0 displayed 19, not account-wide Opals. The source review and plan now correct the earlier Gravel/Opal mislabel.
- **Sediment data:** `SedimentRepo.ts:7–19` defines the ten ordered rows Gravel, Goldust, Redstone, Mythril, Cobaltine, Bruntine, Freezium, Sweetium, Rad Coral, Hyper Coral. `HoleCurrencyRepo` indices 0–9 supply their native game glyphs (`docs/research/hole-part-4-artwork.md`). Fill shows each real sediment image/name, saved stock/capacity and progress. Current assigned bucket count and grouped rate remain tied to actual assignments; known zero rows say “No buckets assigned” and use opacity 0.75. The configured save showed ten buckets assigned to Mythril, nine known-zero rows, and the hypothetical fill estimate remained visible for zero-assignment rows.
- **Expansion cap:** `Holes[8][s]` is current sediment expansion level and capacity uses `100 × 1.5^level × (1 + unlockRequirement / 100)` (`well.tsx:73–80`; game source `240759`). Automatic expansion requires `Holes[11][10] == 1`, with a level-14 gate removed only when global `B_UPG(13,1)` is owned (`idleon1.23.txt:265595–601`). The table shows 14 ticks only when that cap remains; known removal shows “No cap” and current level; unknown cap is not presented as removed. The configured save had verified removal and showed each current level with “No cap.”
- **Full in:** Added `Well.getFullInTime(sediment)` to calculate `(capacity - current) / sum(all owned bucket rates)` from existing prepared per-bucket rates. Game `BucketFillRate` takes bucket index/gold status, not target sediment (`idleon1.23.txt:240760–240779`); current assignment does not change the rate. This is a simple hypothetical reassignment at unchanged ownership, gold status, and bonuses. It does not use auto-expansion toggle, cap, Study overflow conversion, elapsed-time clock, or future expansion forecast. At/above capacity it returns zero (“Full”) before ownership/rate checks. Below capacity unknown stock/capacity/ownership/rate returns Unavailable; known zero total rate returns No production. The explanatory tooltip appears once in the Full in column header. No per-row tooltips or countdowns. Source details are in `docs/research/hole-part-4-source-review.md`.
- **Study:** Well reuses existing Study index 0/owner APIs and mirrors latest Bell layout: level plus static effect and participation/rate on the first row, then progress/current requirement. No studied-by owner, duplicated rate, new Study calculation, or router was added. The configured save showed level 16, Inactive, and `73K / 143K` progress.
- **Focused live check:** `yarn ui:status` reported app/browser ready. Final `yarn ui:run .ui-verification/hole-well-table-bell-border-20261018.cjs` passed on `/world-5/hole`, using the configured save without mutation. Assertions covered ten real sediment rows, no invented Rocks row, header-only Full-in tooltip, 0.75 opacity and retained hypothetical estimates for nine known-zero rows, uncapped counts without misleading 14-tick bars, latest Study index-0 layout without owner wording, and Bell Opals `border-left-width: 0px`. ### Part 4 selected live UI revision — 2026-10-18

A bucket count is unavailable if ownership or assignment for any owned bucket is unknown; partial counts never appear complete. Source-backed expansion timing estimates `max(0, capacity - current stock) / grouped rate` hours. v1.23 source adds production by elapsed hours before checking capacity (`idleon1.23.txt:265588–604`); capacity is `100 × 1.5^level × (1 + unlockRequirement / 100)` (`240759`). Auto-expansion requires `Holes[11][10] == 1`, clamps stock at capacity, and at level 14+ requires owned `B_UPG(13,1)`. It then subtracts `capacity / round(2 + 2 × StudyBolaiaBonuses(0,99))` and increments level. `Well.getExpansionTiming()` reports approximate duration, threshold reached/next update, disabled, capped, no production, unavailable stock, or unknown gate/data. It predicts no countdown, tick cadence, offline time, rate changes, or later expansions. Manual Opal expansion is separate (`263580–589`); no global sediment maximum was established. Harp string letters now use the seven native game glyphs. Lamp stock shows its native Wishes Left glyph. **Evaluator diagnosis follow-up — 2026-10-17:** Retested fresh, owned, isolated runtime and investigated the call through the game-researcher and Node inspector. Prior timeout remains real historical evidence; it did not recur in the controlled fresh-start run.

- Shared port `3204` and PID `61363` remained untouched. Owned port `3217` loaded `latest.json`, character 3 `Sludgev4`; `/status` required `source:"offline"`, `cdpConnected`, `injected`, and `gameReady` true. `/game-version` was `Luminous_Menagerie` 1.23.
- Game-researcher inspected `IdleonWikiBot/codefiles/idleon1.23.txt:240760–779`. `BucketFillRate(t,i)` contains no loop; `i` is unused. It calls the gold helpers, direct `B_UPG`, monument, Lamp, Gambit, measurement, Bell, Jar, stamp and Cglunko helpers, plus Gem Shop and Holes reads. Its internal Fountain cache loops are bounded (`241440–462`). Caller passes bucket index and `0`.
- Safe component calls completed serially before the aggregate: `Fountain_BonTOT(0,15)=3563`; `B_UPG(104,1)=0`; gold owned `1`; gold multiplier `36.63`; B_UPG 58/59/1/26/14/15 outputs `45.57174588754073`, `34.1`, `5`, `5`, `115560`, `1`; monument `15722.24`; Lamp `3203.5`; Gambit `165.88814805249447`; measurement `25881.210653997474`; Bell `9800`; Jar `160`; CavernRes stamp `69`; Cglunko `130`. Saved inputs: Fountain level `285`, Marbleization `2`, schematic 104 `0`, Gem Shop `[2]=10`, Holes `[11][1]=19`, `OptLacc[644]=13`.
- `BucketFillRate(0,0)` returned `100350592397289070` on the first aggregate call after those components. After `/reset`, the immediate call returned the same value. A second fresh process returned the same value. At diagnosis time, the corrected app still returned `613735095697764500`; the operand fix and parity verification are recorded above.
- **Diagnosis limit:** The previously timed-out evaluation was gone when inspected; this run cannot identify its exact renderer frame or pending CDP command. The inspector attach through supplied DAP failed (`debugpy transport ended`). A raw Node Inspector breakpoint confirmed the fresh call reached CDP dispatch and returned, but did not capture a renderer pause. Do not claim that the old timeout's hidden evaluation was a game loop or a specific dependency.
- **Confirmed offline-runtime defect:** `runtime.cjs:9–16,264–274` races `Runtime.evaluate` with a timer but does not cancel the operation. `server.cjs:45–52` releases the request lock when the race rejects; `runtime.cjs:255–261` reports stored readiness, not active evaluator responsiveness. Therefore timeout can leave a zombie CDP evaluation and false-ready `/status`. This explains stale status/lock behavior, not the original trigger. A true fix requires runtime timeout-recovery semantics (cancel/replace evaluator or page); no harness change was made because selected task prohibits permanent runtime edits.
- Evidence: `/tmp/hole-part4-well-hang-diagnosis.txt`. Both owned runtimes were stopped; no permanent instrumentation or repository scripts were retained. Save was read-only; no save refresh/mutation or unrelated service action occurred.


- **Checks at diagnosis time:** `yarn test tests/domains/hole/part4-pipeline.test.ts --runInBand` passed: 1 suite / 4 tests. The rate assertion had been removed because the then-current normal-import output `613735095697764500` did not match the game aggregate. The standalone smoke and regression assertion added in the corrective follow-up above supersede that rate check. No build, lint, formatter, or broader suite ran.
- **Cleanup verification:** Repository search found no remaining Harp `.balance`, per-note `.cost`, or `superbit69Purchased` references. `Harp.noteCost` and `Gaming.getCglunkoBonus()` remain.

### Part 4 phase 5 independent backend review — 2026-10-17

Read-only review of issue #444, the approved backend plan, current Hole implementation and tests, source evidence, backend patterns, and coding standards. No checks were rerun.

**Findings for human decision**

This is an unapproved static-data change, not a missing prerequisite.

The approved plan asks to map 12 saved indices to seven families, but v1.23 source defines no such mapping. Keep unmapped levels unmapped; human decision is needed on the plan requirement. No WikiBot or static-data request follows.

It does not compare Well or Bell outputs, Harp saved tune/layout, note balances/costs or string EXP/levels/passives, or most Lamp outputs and reset targets. Snapshot stability and implementation-derived context assertions do not replace the plan's same-save comparisons. Existing same-save values use `latest.json` under v1.23 assets; its capture version remains unknown.
- **Duplicate Lamp state — `hole.tsx:1620–1640,1664–1666`:** `schematicCostMultiplier` always copies `schematicCostDiscount`, has no consumer, and adds a second mutable representation of the same factor.
- **Player 3 STR remains unattributed:** The selected changes prepare Hole aspect outputs and read Gaming/Divinity values for Well/Bell. Focused diff shows no change to player-stat formulas or `getSchematicBonus`; Bell's new nullable bonus feeds Hole villager EXP, not a direct player-STR calculation. The expected `6578131` is paired v1.22 evidence, while `6796531` is website output under v1.23. ### Part 4 phase 5 independent backend review — 2026-10-17

Read-only review of issue [#444](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/444), the approved [backend plan](hole-part-4-backend-plan.md), current handoff, coding standards, scoped backend changes, existing pipeline interfaces, and focused tests. Checked game-source claims against the assigned v1.23 source and existing static records. No fixes or checks ran.

**Findings for human decision**

- **Unknown versus zero — `well.tsx:69–74`:** Missing sediment stock sets `unavailable` to `false`. A missing value is unknown, not known spendable stock; the separate `current` field is null, but this boolean can still report the opposite of the required unavailable/unknown state.
- **Unknown versus zero — `hole.tsx:1564–1573`:** Missing saved wish levels become zero, and Wish 5 discount is then calculated as 1. The Wish repository guarantees twelve static wish records, not that every imported save contains twelve saved levels. This also makes missing level data look like unpurchased wishes and produces costs from a false zero. The focused test repeats the zero fallback at `part4-pipeline.test.ts:100–102`.
- **Partial Harp outcome — `hole.tsx:1541–1543`:** Harp exposes power/hour, assignments, and permanent string bonuses, but no current assigned-string trigger effects or their random/order-sensitive context. The approved plan requires Harp active/passive effects (`hole-part-4-backend-plan.md:57`) and says to include relevant reward context without promising a fixed result. The source formulas are not a license to simulate rewards; the plan should clarify whether current-state effect/chance outputs are required.
- **Duplicated slot calculation — `hole.tsx:1526–1533` and `1822–1829`:** `Harp.prepare` recomputes slot count and dependencies already calculated by `prepareSharedUpgradeEffects` and fed to schematics. This creates two owners for the same result and risks disagreement; the approved integration names the existing `holeUpgradeEffects` stage as slot preparation.
- **Backend presentation geometry — `hole.tsx:1474,1535–1539`:** `Harp.slotPositions` adds pixel coordinates and breakpoints to the backend. The game source confirms these are renderer positions (`idleon1.23.txt:241025–30`); the approved outcome requires saved layout/slots, not screen coordinates. This is frontend overfitting and premature presentation work.
- **Impossible static-record guard — `hole.tsx:1573,1598`:** `this.wishes[5]` is guarded despite `LampWishRepo` supplying all twelve records and the aspect building wishes from that repository. This conflicts with `DATA-01`; use direct access for required initialized records.
- **Unused Harp input — `hole.tsx:1478,1488,1524–1525`:** Harp stores Gaming's Cglunko bonus but does not consume it in any approved current-state result. Source uses Cglunko in reward production, which this work excludes. Keep the owner getter for Well; remove Harp-only state unless a required result consumes it.

**Verification and limits:** `part4-pipeline.test.ts:65–126` checks save-to-field copies, fixed counts, and wallet ownership; `:128–147` checks repeat-import snapshot stability. These tests do not assert calculation outputs against independent game values. The plan requires zero-tolerance, same-save v1.23 parity for these calculations. ### Part 4 source-meaning and verification-route research — 2026-10-17

**Source-confirmed answers**

- **Negative Well stock:** v1.23 source does not define a player-facing cause or mechanic name for negative mapped stock. The drawing branch displays `|` below zero and blocks bucket reassignment for that sediment (261631–45, 263560–79). Treat `|` only as an unavailable marker, not a quantity or explanation. Do not claim its cause is known.
- **Bell modes:** Source labels the selected activity Ringing, Pinging, Cleaning, or Renewing. Saved selector `[11][19]` is constrained by `BellTypesOwned`: the UI permits increment while selected mode is below that result and decrement above zero (264217–41). The display indexes four labels and rate/XP calculations by the saved selection (262024–57). This proves four game modes and the unlock-gated selector, not that a particular save owns all four. Do not infer saved mode access from a fixed four-mode UI list.
- **Harp passives:** Game UI explicitly says level bonuses “are permanent and work at all times,” while assigned slots separately determine which strings claim on a Harp trigger (261980–96, 264267–301, 265615–20). Thus ownership/level passive effects are not conditioned on current layout; assignment controls strum-trigger rewards. Keep unassigned owned-string passives in outcomes. The Wiki's [The Caverns](https://idleon.wiki/wiki/The_Caverns) page likewise describes string-type unlocks and level-up from plucking, but source text is formula authority.
- **Wish 1 resets:** Source shows the selectable names and maps each branch to its exact mutation (261793–804, 269743–65). Those are source-confirmed write effects, not read-only outcomes observed by exercising a reset. A runtime check would mutate save state and is not required to state the documented branch; do not trigger one on an imported player save.

**Runtime route and observed limits**

- Checked the canonical default `http://127.0.0.1:3101`: `GET /status` reported `source:"offline"` and all readiness fields true; `GET /game-version` reported `Luminous_Menagerie`, version `1.23`. This establishes exact local game-asset version, not latest online version or real-save provenance.
- Read-only `/exec` inspection on that offline server reported selected character index 3, Hole state `[0][3] = -1`, Well stock had no negative entries, Bell selection `[11][19] = 0`, and Wish 5 level `[21][5] = 0`. This selected offline state is not an active Hole context; it does not establish a player's available Bell modes, negative-stock meaning, or a matched save/output pair. No mutations were made.
- Feasible route: use offline server by default, verify same-server `/status` readiness and `/game-version`, then read-only `/exec` expressions for the relevant function results and save inputs. For valid parity, pair an explicitly identified v1.23 save with output from that same save and matching active character; extraction outputs alone do not establish fixture provenance (canonical [game-access policy](../../tests/helpers/README.md#game-access), lines 5–20, 80–88, 118–120, 184–193). Existing helpers can write result-only outputs elsewhere with `--output-dir`; they do not refresh the fixture unless `--refresh-save` is requested. Never use that flag without a human-requested refresh and a ready live server.
No version-matched `docs/game-snippets` capture or matched v1.23 save/output parity was obtained. The runtime observations above establish only offline asset version/readiness and the inspected character state. Static source and generated exports remain distinct from runtime parity; see the current correction above for the resolved static-data prerequisite error.

### Part 3 selected backend revisions after Phase 5 review — 2026-10-16

- **Remaining-resource semantics:** `ResourceCavren.prepare` now sets `resourceRemaining` to `Math.max(0, resourceRequirement - resourceCurrent)`. `resourceCurrent` stays raw, and `resourceRequirementMet` remains its independent threshold comparison. Hive stays ready when overfilled; no surplus field was added.
- **DATA-01:** `getResourceRequirementReduction` directly indexes required initialized Study objects and directly accesses all four initialized caverns. It retains `levelAvailable`, missing collectible-save, and nullable saved `layerState` checks. Removed optional object chaining and Study non-null assertions.
- **Regression:** Added below/at/above requirement cases to `tests/domains/hole/part3-pipeline.test.ts`. They assert raw current, requirement, non-negative outstanding amount, and readiness.
- **Focused runtime smoke:** This command exited **0** and exercised production `ResourceCavren.prepare` directly:

  ```text
  yarn tsx -e 'import { ResourceCavren } from "./data/domain/world-5/hole/hole"; const cavern = new ResourceCavren(0, "Motherload", "Ores"); const requirement = 200 * Math.pow(2.2, 1); for (const current of [requirement - 1, requirement, requirement + 1]) { cavern.layerState = 0; cavern.resourceCurrent = current; cavern.prepare(0); const remaining = Math.max(0, requirement - current); const met = current >= requirement; if (cavern.resourceCurrent !== current || cavern.resourceRequirement !== requirement || cavern.resourceRemaining !== remaining || cavern.resourceRequirementMet !== met) throw new Error(JSON.stringify({ current, requirement: cavern.resourceRequirement, remaining: cavern.resourceRemaining, met: cavern.resourceRequirementMet })); console.log(JSON.stringify({ current: cavern.resourceCurrent, requirement: cavern.resourceRequirement, remaining: cavern.resourceRemaining, met: cavern.resourceRequirementMet })); }'
  ```

  Output: current `439.00000000000006`, requirement `440.00000000000006`, remaining `1`, ready `false`; equality remaining `0`, ready `true`; above requirement remaining `0`, ready `true`. An initial disposable assertion incorrectly expected the floating-point requirement to equal literal `440`; correcting the smoke to use the formula's actual result passed. No production expectation or tolerance changed.

The new regression test is therefore authored but not separately executed. The focused direct smoke does not establish full-pipeline health.

Its active-Doot branch replaces ordinary selected-god text with: “The Divine feel their obsolescence, and grant +{allStatPercent}% ALL STAT for all your characters... may your stats loom large!” The original `Companions(0)` gate excludes the active character below Divinity level two. Existing prepared `PocketDivinityUpgrade.characters[].allStatPercent` supplies the accepted eligible-character amount. Do not restore character selection, alignment/eligibility inspection, or union/overlap lists.
- **Display exposure, not mechanics:** A small Pocket-owned accessor may expose the common known amount already prepared for eligible records. It must not select an arbitrary character or add the formula in React. Missing safe prepared amount remains **Benefit unavailable**, not zero. The full Doot sentence lives in the original display caller, not the supplied generated Pocket description. It is source evidence, not a new handwritten production description. Full localized prose would require normal static extraction if requested; compact typed-value presentation needs no generated change.

**Resolved schematic artwork:** The same evidence records the original `_customEvent_HoleStuffz` caller, original availability order, all **105 released identity mappings**, and **110/110 successful PNG requests**, including five optional game backgrounds. Every native asset is **97×97** and its CDN payload matches the local SHA-256. Foreground identity is `HoleUIbuildUpg{stableBuildingId}`; `squareImageType` selects a separate background. Slot 2 is stable ID 58. Bee String is stable ID **31**, slot **105**, and remains excluded. Stable ID **105** is released. The plan uses verified foregrounds on this website's dark card surface, not copied colored game backgrounds.

- **Display exposure:** Propose initialization-owned `Schematic.getImageData()` metadata only. Existing static identity is sufficient. No generated field, copied catalogue, WikiBot action, public image, runtime URL construction, or formula change is required.

The initial scout's missing-source/art claims are not blockers or authoritative conclusions.

**Control decision:** **Show Owned starts checked.** This preserves the prior all-visible catalogue and the UI guide's conservative default. The unchecked state hides only known built entries. No new formula, authored effect, or audit panel is needed.

### Part 2 phase 5 fresh review after H40–H41

**Authority and method (2026-10-15):** Fresh independent, read-only review covers selected part 2 [#442](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/442), its published plan approval, the unchanged [backend plan](hole-part-2-backend-plan.md), and the approved specification. Read coding standards, contributor guidance, domain guidance, Hole vocabulary, architecture guidance, UI guidance for the affected Research display, and the phase 5 workflow. Isolated `code-review` axes ran serially: `HolePart2ConsumerFreshReview.HoleConsumerStandards`, then `HolePart2ConsumerFreshReview.HoleConsumerSpec`. Plan approval was not treated as a standards exception.

**Comparison scope:** Reviewed the complete selected uncommitted backend against `HEAD` (`31f1513731defa60a854125390f268924bcd9db9`), not only H40–H41. Coverage includes Hole/Villager/Gambit, registration, Companions, Divinity, Tome, Farming, Orion/Poppy, relevant Research backend/display changes, eight selected-part suites and helper, the changed part 1 pipeline suite, four config/result pairs, and affected consumers. Supplied generated building/currency exports were read-only prerequisites. Unrelated Research/Spelunking work and the unrelated Divinity blessing test edit were not attributed to this revision.

#### Standards

**Zero new findings.** No additional documented breach or actionable maintainability smell was confirmed. H36–H39 retain aspect-owned progression, boundary translation, prepared Doot eligibility, and missing-schematic meaning. H40's nullable state follows a genuine optional-input dependency; initialized owners remain required. The narrow Research contributor type preserves numeric contracts for independent contributors. No speculative engine, frontend layout contract, unnecessary absence guard, or manufactured effect description was added. `Unavailable` is a missing-value status, not replacement game-mechanic prose.

#### Specification

**Zero new selected findings. H40–H41 are resolved within their inspected contracts.** Measurement 13 now supplies its nullable effective benefit to Gambit. Dependent scores, unlocks, rewards, Orion/Poppy inputs, Orion production, and Research offline totals retain unavailable state. Saved records, independent production/contributors, Tutorial-gated rewards, and known zeroes remain distinct. Research's existing display handles the changed contract without new layout or interaction; this is necessary consumer migration under plan lines 65 and 74, not another feature's UI delivery. No rendered-behavior claim is added.

Divinity owns eligible ordinary activation. Pocket consumes that authority for overlap and effective membership. Secondary eligibility requires a primary link and an unlocked secondary god. Effective membership deduplicates ordinary links, prepared Doot, and Pocket. Primary-only Gaming remains separate. Early shared effects and late inspection retain their distinct roles. #### Specification

- **H40 — P2: Missing Measurement inputs lose availability in Gambit.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:1966` supplies unavailable Death Note points when optional character `KLA_` input is missing. `Measurement.prepare` records unavailable statistics but stores a zero fallback at line 922. Measurement 13 correctly exposes `effectiveBenefit = null`. The Gambit consumer at lines 1982–1987 still calls `getBonus()`, which uses that fallback and returns the purchased base benefit with multiplier one. It publishes a numeric multiplier, total score, and unlock decisions despite the missing dependency (`gambit.tsx:145–153`). The plan, line 65, requires: **“A genuinely missing optional save input makes only dependent values unavailable.”** Its integration also requires migrating affected consumers when an API changes. **Recommendation for decision:** Preserve Measurement availability through the affected Gambit results and consumers. Keep independent saved challenge progress and verified zeroes. Add a narrow real-input omission/recovery contract using existing test conventions. This is a static code-path finding, not an observed game branch.
- **H41 — P2: Pocket overlap counts an ineligible secondary link as ordinary activation.** `hole.tsx:263–281` includes every saved `esGod` in `ordinaryGodIds`, then uses that set for overlap, added gods, and effective gods. Captured original [`hole-part2-god-callers-1.22.json`](../../.ui-verification/hole-part2-backend-20261014/hole-part2-god-callers-1.22.json), `result[0].source`, shows the secondary `Bonus_MAJOR` path requires a primary link and an unlocked secondary god (`Divinity[25] > selected god ID`). Without effective Doot, an ineligible saved secondary selection therefore appears active. If Pocket selects that god, inspection calls it overlap instead of a new activation. The plan, line 61, requires: **“Preserve secondary-link rules and callers that count only primary links.”** **Recommendation for decision:** Obtain eligible ordinary activation from its Divinity owner. Keep the saved secondary identity separate, then calculate Pocket overlap from effective links. Existing secondary parsing predates this patch; this finding concerns the new inspection records, not a claimed legacy parsing regression. The paired Doot context does not exercise this branch.

**Specification verdict:** Two P2 findings remain, H40–H41. H39 is resolved for its selected missing-schematic-row contract. Otherwise, inspected formulas and retained comparisons support schematic order/currency/prices, the shared Majik pool/caps/effects, and all 17 Measurement mappings and benefits. Early preparation precedes required consumers; late inspection does not feed shared calculations. Descriptions use generated text and current-value substitution. No UI, forecast, allocation model, or new character-stat engine was added.

### Part 2 phase 5 independent backend review

**Authority and method (2026-10-15):** Fresh, read-only review covers selected part 2 [#442](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/442), its published approval, the [backend plan](hole-part-2-backend-plan.md), and the [approved specification](hole-spec-draft.md). Followed phase 5 of the [feature workflow](../human/feature-workflow.md), `code-review`, architecture guidance, [CODING_STANDARDS.md](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md), [CONTRIBUTING.md](../../CONTRIBUTING.md), domain discovery, and existing Hole vocabulary. Isolated axes ran serially: `HolePart2BackendReview.HoleP2Standards`, then `HolePart2BackendReview.HoleP2Spec`. Plan approval was not treated as a standards exception.

**Comparison scope:** Baseline `HEAD` is `31f1513731defa60a854125390f268924bcd9db9`. Selected backend changes are uncommitted. Reviewed `git diff HEAD --` for Hole, Villager, pipeline registration, Divinity, Companions, Tome, Farming, and the affected part 1 pipeline test. Also reviewed the eight new selected-part suites, helper, extraction configs/results, and relevant producer/consumer contracts. Supplied generated building/currency exports were read-only prerequisite context, not attributed to the implementation worker. Unrelated Research, Spelunking, navigation, generated exports, and unrelated test edits were excluded.

#### Standards

- **H36 — P2, MODEL-01: Majiks owns individual upgrade progression behavior.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:314–326` computes and writes each `CosmoUpgrade`'s initial/enhancement levels, access, cap, predecessor state, and purchase eligibility inside `Majiks.prepare`. The upgrade stores these fields but cannot prepare its own progression. A rule change spans both the container and responsible concept. [MODEL-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#model-01--model-responsible-concepts) requires aspect update logic and game calculations on the aspect class; the plan's responsibilities at lines 21–22 do not waive that rule. **Recommendation for decision:** Let `CosmoUpgrade` prepare its progression from the pool, access, gate, and predecessor inputs supplied by `Majiks`.
- **H37 — P2, PARSE-01: Raw Majik tree encoding survives parsing.** `hole.tsx:293–297` retains the original save arrays in `investments`. Calculation later converts their values with `Number` at line 308 and reads predecessors through `[tree][treePosition - 1]` at line 322. The calculation still depends on raw save layout, despite parsed upgrade records. This violates [PARSE-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#parse-01--translate-at-the-boundary) and the plan's line 35 boundary. **Recommendation for decision:** Translate predecessor state and complete numeric investment inputs during parsing, including undisplayed placeholder investments. Calculate from named state rather than retained save arrays.
- **H38 — P3, non-blocking smell judgement: Pocket inspection repeats the Doot eligibility rule.** `hole.tsx:235–241` recomputes companion ownership plus the character's Divinity-level gate. `data/domain/companions.tsx:121–125` already prepares that rule as `PlayerDivinityInfo.dootActive`, which `data/domain/world-5/divinity.tsx:222–226` consumes. Late Pocket inspection runs after both updates. This is **Duplicated Code / possible Feature Envy**, not a separate hard violation. Two production owners must now maintain the same gate. **Recommendation for decision:** Consume prepared eligibility for existing Divinity records; retain genuine missing-save handling.

**Standards verdict:** Two documented P2 breaches remain, plus one P3 maintainability judgement. No further substantiated scope expansion, speculative framework, frontend-shaped state, manufactured description, or impossible initialized-object absence guard was found.

#### Specification

- **H39 — P2: Missing schematic input becomes a false zero Contented Creator benefit.** `hole.tsx:1467` counts built flags and supplies a plain numeric total to `Majiks.prepare`. If optional `Holes[13]` is absent while Village Majik investment exists, `Schematic.parse` sets all flags false (`352–354`). `CosmoUpgrade.getEffects` then exposes a known `0%` benefit (`163–164`), and `getDescription` substitutes that false total (`187`). Saved Majik progression remains known; its schematic-dependent benefit does not. Existing villager growth already marks this dependency unavailable (`1620,1644,1671`). The backend plan, line 65, requires: **“A genuinely missing optional save input makes only dependent values unavailable. Retain independent progression and known zeroes.”** The product specification, line 48, likewise says **“Unknown inputs must not become zero.”** **Recommendation for decision:** Preserve construction-count availability and leave the dependent effect/placeholder unavailable without hiding saved Majik progression. Current complete-save tests do not exercise this path.

**Specification verdict:** One P2 defect remains. Apart from H39, inspected implementation and assertions support released schematic order/availability, full currency joins, current price formulas, the shared Majik pool and caps, Pocket deduplication, and all 17 Measurement mappings, units, and formulas. Early shared preparation precedes affected consumers; late inspection does not feed shared calculations. Tome and Farming producer changes serve required numeric statistics, not another feature. Descriptions derive from generated static text and verified placeholder materialization. No new UI, allocation model, forecast, character-stat engine, or unrelated feature was introduced.

Initial failures and their recorded corrections remain historical evidence.

Assertions retain saved progression, imported main stats, character binding, stable derived records, and affected Hole/Divinity/Companion/Votes/Equinox outputs. Producer comparisons cover required numeric Tome, crop, Death Note, damage, Slab, Study, Lengthmeister, and Fountain inputs.
- **Sufficiency:** Retained evidence supports personalized parity for one matched real save and original offline Jelly_Operator v1.22, active Player 3 only. Pairing records all **799** fixture keys equal. Eleven imported character bindings do not establish eleven active runtime contexts. Source inspection supports rules, not unobserved personalized parity.
- **Unobserved branches remain explicit:** Nonzero Lamp discount/floor; low-Kaipu locked or affordable unbuilt released schematics; placeholder investment; disabled enhancement or unmet predecessor; nonzero Profoun-tain; alternate/duplicate Pocket selections, absent Doot, below-two Divinity, or secondary-link Pocket overlap; the lower Measurement multiplier branch; locked or zero-level Measurements. ### Phase 8 amended UI independent review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-14):** Reviewed selected part 1 [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441) in a fresh independent session. The current amended [UI plan](hole-part-1-ui-plan.md), its [publication](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441#issuecomment-6160185301), and approved rendered captures govern this review. Earlier owner-link, URL-selection, contributor-table, and automatic scroll/focus requirements are superseded. Followed the scoped UI guide, `code-review`, Impeccable context, `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, and domain vocabulary discovery. Standards and specification assessments used separate agents in serial order. Approval was not treated as a standards exception.

Reviewed current uncommitted production content with `git diff f9efbcf5 -- app/world-5/hole/content.tsx`. The reviewed content SHA-256 is `e0193d66c398b7caf71e9ad72e1bad1f07f66792cd881424ee1d2e8e61f88902`, matching the retained production evidence source. The route wrapper and both accepted Hole backend sources have no diff from `41f29730`. Unrelated Research and Spelunking work is excluded.

#### Standards

- **H31 — P3, CONSIST-01: Labeled row fields bypass DataRow's label ownership.** `content.tsx:220–223,275–276` uses `kind: "content", label: null` for Level, Invested Opals, stored progress, and rate fields, then renders labels inside `TextAndLabel`. The row guide reserves `label: null` for genuinely unlabeled content (`ui-rows.md:16–17`) and assigns compact label/value typography to DataRow (`24–26`). The UI guide also distinguishes wrappers outside DataRow from its private row rendering (`ui-patterns.md:101–104`). The result is readable and matches the approved preview, but shared row-label changes will not reach these fields. Use text fields for simple values and labeled content fields for genuinely rich values. Keep the selected progression grouping and tooltip behavior. The plan's stronger value hierarchy does not exempt field composition from the shared contract.
- **H32 — P3, DATA-01 / SIMPLE-01: UI revalidates guaranteed Statue contributor metadata.** `content.tsx:52–56` checks contributor count, owner encoding, value identity, unit, and combination. `Villager.prepareCharacterRates` always appends exactly one Statue contributor with those fixed fields (`villager.tsx:341–369`), including when saved Statue input is missing. This duplicates the producer contract in the view and makes a metadata change require another maintenance step. Remove guaranteed-structure validation, not genuine missing-value/reason checks or the complete numeric comparison required by the plan. The typed-input `typeof` checks and the post-completeness null-input recheck are redundant defenses in the same concern. Finite-value checks, nullable saved inputs, and the local account-loading guard remain justified.

#### Specification

- **H33 — P2: Useful nonzero growth modifiers are no longer inspectable.** The whitelist at `content.tsx:201–209` retains only purchased EXP, role multiplier, Equal Spread, Opal Enthusiasm, and the bundle, besides the separately shown Statue. All other shared growth sources disappear, with no surviving shared context. The approved plan says **“Keep useful nonzero modifiers”** (`ui-plan.md:17`) and retains **“useful advanced context”** in decision 5 (`70`). Real-save records include Bravery **8,314.38%**, Grimoire **172.5%**, Arcade **9.23%**, and other material contributors. The full-size `rate-0-rounded.png` shows that none appear. Players can read the rate but cannot inspect those sources. Preserve useful nonzero shared modifiers once in concise tooltip context. Do not restore repeated contributor tables, owner metadata, zeros, or neutral multipliers. The approved preview contains the same omission; visual agreement alone does not satisfy the written outcome.

#### All fifteen presentation decisions

| Decision | Assessment and evidence |
|---|---|
| 1. Concise game terms | Met. Progression, participation, and benefit copy no longer exposes owner, intrinsic, scope, or combination labels. |
| 2. Toolbox priorities only | Met. Adjacent values, recognizable art, and benefit clarity carry over. No competitor topology, colors, ETA sorting, cards, or total rate was added. |

| 4. No deep links or source surfaces | Met. Router/query state, owner destinations, source panels, return links, and feature anchors are removed. The retained legacy-query record proves local selection does not write the URL. |
| 5. Advanced rate tooltips | Partial: H33. Growth details buttons are removed and conventional tooltips work, but useful shared modifiers are discarded. |
| 6. Deduplication | Met for repeated descriptions/effect tables, zero source/location rows, neutral modifiers, repeated status, and contributor inventories. H33 identifies over-pruning, not a request to restore those inventories. |
| 7. Value hierarchy | Visually met. Aligned progression groups and stronger values match the approved render. H31 remains a separate shared-row standards issue. |
| 8. Statue-bound character meaning | Met in the supported evidence. Complete unrounded records agree across all 55 villager/character contexts, yielding immediate rates for all 11 imported characters. Differing/unavailable fixtures retain truthful ranges, optional named inspection, matched Statue context, and visible missing-input reasons. No account-wide rate claim appears. |
| 9. Useful Lantern locations | Met. Stock is 37, storage is the only nonzero baseline location, uses are 0 / 12, and the effect is +2 hours. Missing location inputs remain unavailable. Alternate/zero/missing locations are view fixtures. |
| 10. No wiki/manual prose | Met. Only concise progression, benefit, reset, and exclusion context remains. |
| 11. No automatic scroll/focus | Met. Source has no destination effects. All 38 retained native-click transitions preserve actual shared scrollers or record natural height clamping and blur. |
| 12. Players/obols rails | Visually met. Both Hole rails use a padded 25% column, shared selection arrow/background, and dark-1 panel. Reference locator timeouts remain explicit below. |
| 13. Verified art | Met. All five CDN banners loaded at 303×64 and clip their verified 56×64 artwork region. Opal/Lantern metadata is reused. Unverified cavern thumbnails are not guessed. |
| 14. Local-only cavern benefits | Met. All 18 cavern identities remain reachable and show only their own Study benefit, local earned Opals, and one website-support notice. |
| 15. Contextual numeric benefits | Met. The 18 Study rows preserve values, relevant conditions, and missing reasons without divisor/unit/scope schema. Cross-skilling arithmetic stays in tooltips and does not claim a literal resource reduction. |

#### Standards

- **H27 — P3, LANG-01: Player-facing details expose implementation terms instead of their meaning.** `app/world-5/hole/content.tsx:269-271` first translates the effect type, then prints raw `owned-study-contribution` and `owned-study-rule` scope tokens. It also renders domain notes verbatim. Actual Fountain detail `12-study-16-detail-expanded.png` says “The delivered marbleization caller checks upgrade exclusions without a Study check.” Wisdom detail `12-study-13-detail-expanded.png` refers to “The round caller.” Cove detail and its owner viewport repeat raw scope tokens. Other authored labels expose “typed effects,” “The backend floors,” and raw owner tokens (`content.tsx:286,338,370,396,430`). These require knowledge of implementation internals, contrary to LANG-01. Show readable scope and source names. Explain the established player-facing restrictions without caller/backend terminology. Preserve the divisor rule, Wisdom capacity condition, and Fountain owned-effect versus full eligibility distinction; do not replace them with a stronger game-benefit claim or change accepted calculations.
- **H28 — P3, DATA-01: The saved-time object has an impossible absence branch.** `content.tsx:171` casts `timeAway` as `| undefined` and uses `?.GlobalTime` after the loaded-data gate. `data/domain/idleonData.tsx:419` creates that map entry unconditionally; line `423` directly accesses the parsed object's `GlobalTime` before the import completes. `lib/stores/appDataStore.tsx:191-192,207-208` publishes `StaticData`/`LiveData` only after that import. The outer object is therefore required on this path. Remove only its artificial optionality. Retain checks for a genuinely missing or invalid saved timestamp field. This is a maintainability finding, not a demonstrated wrong timestamp.
- **H29 — P3, READ-01: The Study effect field combines too many view responsibilities in one expression.** `content.tsx:393` combines the effect loop, two semantic-caveat branches, detail selection/navigation, and the owner link inside one field expression. Lines `276-277` similarly mix pinned-owner inclusion, access filtering, description materialization, effect-label search, and normalization. Correct behavior still leaves these independently meaningful rules difficult to inspect or change. Use explicit named view steps or a small local summary renderer. No new generic framework, options layer, or broad component migration is needed. This finding concerns responsibility clarity, not line length or formatter-enforced style.

#### Specification

- **H30 — P2: Exact owner links open content without bringing the owner into view.** The plan's lines `60,66` require a cavern link back to its exact Study detail. `content.tsx:175-187` routes every internal owner link with `scroll: false`, without a fragment or a destination-scroll/focus step. Studies still render all preceding rows (`386-420`). The production `09-studies-actual-first-viewport.png` shows only Studies 1–3; Cove's Study 18 return link appears near the bottom of `13-cove-owner-actual-viewport.png`. Selecting it opens Study 18 below 17 preceding rows instead of presenting the selected detail. The full-ledger owner has the same issue below five villager rows. This is source-confirmed missing destination presentation; the exact post-click viewport consequence is inferred from the code and retained composition, not freshly reproduced. The retained scenario checks `target.waitFor({ state: 'visible' })` at `scenario.cjs:136-145,353-361,492-494`; Playwright visibility permits offscreen elements. Those passing assertions establish rendered/query selection, not owner discoverability. Bring the selected owner into view after rendering, including direct query links and reload, without changing the catalogue or adding a new navigation model.

### Phase 4 evidence and stop

**Historical paused-phase record (2026-10-13):** The supervisor approved all five villager rates and contributors for every named imported Player context. The [amended backend-plan publication](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441#issuecomment-6141009754) preserved the initial approval and publication. The evidence below describes the earlier stop and preserved draft, before fresh continuation authority. The [continuation result](#phase-4-continuation-result) supersedes its unfinished-work status, not its recorded observations or check limits.

- **Mechanic source:** Read-only `POST http://127.0.0.1:3101/exec` with JSON `{ "expression": "..." }` returned original **Jelly_Operator 1.22**. In `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Holes.toString()`, `VillagerExpPerHour` spans approximately UTF-16 offsets 2469–4916 and directly multiplies by `1 + StatueBonusGiven28 / 100`. In `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_12._customBlock_ArbitraryCode.toString()`, search `"StatueBonusGiven" == e.substring(0,16)` around offset 30623. That branch reads the current runtime `StatueLevels`. The website owner is [Statue.getBonus(player)](../../data/domain/world-1/statues.tsx), lines 60–121; `PlayerStatues` retains `playerID`, lines 160–176. These are source findings, not observed rate divergence between characters.
- **Character-context contract:** The Statue multiplier affects every villager's growth rate. Different character Statue state can therefore produce valid differences for identical shared Hole progression. Join each rate and contributor set through existing Player ID/name and matching PlayerStatues. Identify that character's Statue percentage and factor separately from account contributors. Expose no arbitrary, best, first, or active primary rate, and no unavailable account-rate field solely because rates disagree. No imported game active-character selector was established. A genuinely missing required input affects only dependent context values; retain independent information and a reason, never fake zero. Keep initialized required objects required. Later UI must label inspection explicitly, such as **Rates for [character name]** or **Inspected character: [name]**. Inspection describes the imported snapshot, not game selection, activity, or assignment changes. Widgets and default inspection selection remain future UI decisions. No forecast, simulation, recommendation, or game action is added.
- **Preserved code state and judgment:** The stopped implementation changed only [villager.tsx](../../data/domain/world-5/hole/villager.tsx). Its saved-state parsing, rescue state, role requirements, readiness, growth-input types, and `VillagerIndex.Bolaia` naming remain preserved drafts, not newly accepted code. `data/domain/world-5/hole/villager.tsx:25-30` defines `VillagerCharacterRate` with `playerId`, `playerName`, `expPerHour`, and `contributors`; that data-only shape fits named-context output. Lines `60-63` declare singular `expPerHour`, `rateUnavailableReason`, `characterRates`, and context-free `contributors`. The singular rate, singular reason, and context-free contributor fields require removal or reframing under the amendment after approval. Lines `76-79` only reset these fields; no implemented rate-disagreement handling branch exists. `calculateExpRate` at `114-119` has no explicit Statue input/factor and no preparation caller; this is unintegrated and unverified, not established personalized arithmetic. The duplicate `Villager` in `hole.tsx` and its `VillagerIndex.Bolai` caller migration remain unfinished. No Hole integration, pipeline update, caller migration, tests, or configs landed. This is not a runnable or accepted backend.
- **Plan-revision source trace:** `data/domain/player.tsx:128-130` supplies existing Player identity. `data/domain/world-1/statues.tsx:60-121` owns `Statue.getBonus(player)`; `160-176` retains and initializes PlayerStatues identity; `218-271` prepares Statue modifiers. Match Statue index 28 within the same PlayerStatues. `data/domain/idleonData.tsx:345` runs `statueBuffs`; `355-358` settles Players and talent contexts before `postPostProcessingMap` begins at `371`. The existing late Hole display-preparation design consumes those settled character inputs together with shared stamps/Sushi inputs. This source trace supports ownership and ordering, not game parity.
- **Preserved outcomes and revision limits:** The amendment retains all four #441 outcomes: five-villager progression/growth/unlocks, the complete Opal ledger/allocation/reset context, all 18 Studies, and Lantern locations/account usage. All other responsibilities, prerequisites, integration, and verification scope remain required, including early Study effects, Tome's cavern-only total, caller migration, and repeated-update safety. ### Phase 5 independent backend review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-13):** Reviewed selected part 1, [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441), against the amended backend plan. Followed [phase 5](../human/feature-workflow.md#5-review-the-backend-independently), `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, and domain vocabulary discovery. Reviewed uncommitted Hole work against `HEAD` (`989abd2d`), including all six new suites/configs/results and their helper. Standards and specification reviews used separate reviewers, delegated serially. Unrelated Research, Deathnote, Gambit, and other feature edits were not classified as Hole scope expansion.

#### Standards

- **H1 — P2, MODEL-01: Villager does not own its complete growth preparation.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:1199-1276` computes role-specific growth rules, availability, and contributor records. `villager.tsx:185-198` receives those results and performs the final multiplication. Polonai and Cosmo role rules therefore remain in the parent. This splits one aspect's behavior across two owners, despite the plan's Villager responsibility. Keep shared inputs and allocation in Hole. Put role-specific preparation and contributor construction on Villager; no new role framework is needed.
- **H2 — P3, DATA-01: Required Pristine Charm has an impossible absence fallback.** `hole.tsx:1590,1621` uses `pristineCharm?.unlocked` to substitute zero when the record is absent. Sneaking creates every delivered Pristine Charm during parsing (`world-6/sneaking.tsx:457-466`). Candy Cache, item ID 21, is a required catalogue record (`data/NinjaItemRepo.ts:837-845`). Treat that record as required. Keep the real unowned-charm branch, not a catalogue-absence branch.
- **H3 — P3, UPDATE-01: Early shared preparation includes display-only calculations.** `hole.tsx:1051-1068` prepares next intrinsic unlocks and the Another Try inspection summary in `postProcessingMap` (`idleonData.tsx:273`). These outputs have no calculation consumers. The standard places UI-only calculations in `postPostProcessingMap`. Keep early Study/schematic inputs and Tome's cavern total early. Prepare unlock/reset inspection data in the existing late Hole step.

These are documented-standard findings, not heuristic smell claims. No generic modifier framework, new account-data model, excessive test seam, or independently actionable abstraction smell was confirmed.

#### Specification

- **H4 — P2: Unknown Superbit ownership becomes inactive neighbor rates.** `hole.tsx:1617` passes a Boolean ownership value without saved-input availability. `gaming/palette.ts:41-49` defaults absent `Gaming[12]` to unpurchased. With a known target and accessible neighbors, Hole then publishes no eligible neighbors and zero neighbor accrual. This violates outcome 3 and the plan's missing-input rule. Preserve the known target rate. Mark only potentially eligible neighbor participation/rates unavailable when ownership input is genuinely missing.
- **H5 — P2: Missing Majik state becomes zero allocation effects.** `hole.tsx:1173-1184` checks villager inputs but not the required Village Majik state. Parsing absent `Holes[5]` assigns Majik levels zero (`1402-1404`). Equal Spread and Opal Enthusiasm then report zero with no missing-input reason, although growth rates correctly become unavailable. This violates outcome 2. Preserve known least investment and invested tens. Make the dependent percentages unavailable when their Majik input is missing.
- **H6 — P2: Missing card save still produces complete growth rates.** The growth availability list (`hole.tsx:1376-1387`) omits `Cards0`, while growth consumes Gloomie card level (`1196,1625`). Cards defaults an absent save to `{}` and missing counts to zero (`cards.tsx:228-247`). A genuinely absent card save can therefore publish numeric rates and a zero card contributor with no reason. This violates outcome 1. Track card-save availability and mark dependent rates/contributions unavailable; retain a real zero from a present save.
- **H7 — P2: Cross-skilling effect names describe the wrong numeric meaning.** `Study.getEffects()` (`hole.tsx:379-385`) labels `bonus * completedLayers` as a percentage requirement reduction. Captured values are 570%, 1870%, 3420%, and 972%. Original `Holes/TotalOreREQ` divides requirements by `1 + (collectible bonus + summed Study contributions) / 100`. These values are additive denominator bonuses, not literal percentage reductions. The config's `study_effects_1/7/11/16` expressions reproduce that intermediate arithmetic, so passing tests do not validate the label. Name the owned divisor contribution accurately and explain its additive combination. Do not claim a complete cavern requirement or invent a new calculator.

H4–H6 are source-proven missing-input branches, not exercised runtime failures. H7 concerns the observed API values and their source-backed meaning. The approved plan does not exempt these contracts from standards or permit unknown inputs to become zero.

#### Evidence assessment and check limits

- The retained comparisons support all five growth rates for Player 3, direct parameters, ledger reconciliation, and the observed Study/Lantern state. Other characters have identity/Statue binding checks, not captured game-output parity. Fresh/repeated imports use the same save; they do not prove changed-account replacement.
- Reviewed early Study/schematic ordering, late settled account/Player/Statue inputs, Tome's cavern-only contract, and all caller migrations. No additional ordering failure was demonstrated. Full ledger attribution and the separate Emperor/schematic floor adjustment follow the inspected refresh caller.
- Study 15 correctly separates owned descriptive text from cavern permission. Original `CanWeBUY_Marbleize` does not check Study ownership. Wisdom's extra caller condition is recorded. Hand-composed effect oracles remain limited evidence for consumer semantics, as H7 shows.
- Jar initialization and Farming's Exotic Market upgrade 51 fix support required inputs and stay within the approved plan. No Hole UI, forecast, simulation, recommendation, store, endpoint, or game action was added. Five-role, 18-Study, and all-character collection coverage serves the approved outcomes; the test count alone is not an excess finding.
- This review read code, diffs, retained artifacts, and original source-function strings from the documented offline endpoint. It ran no tests, builds, lint, formatters, new calculation extraction, game actions, character switches, or time advancement. Phase 4's reported 17 suites / 427 tests, type check, and scoped lint were not rerun. Its test files were ignored by lint. The unobserved branches listed in the continuation result remain unobserved.
- **Decision boundary:** Four specification findings and three standards findings await decisions. Code stayed read-only. Only this handoff changed. No fix, acceptance, tracker publication, commit, push, PR, deployment, or issue closure occurred.

### Phase 5 fresh backend review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-13):** Independently reviewed selected part 1, [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441), against the amended backend plan and its two published plan comments. Reviewed current uncommitted work against `HEAD` (`989abd2d`), including new tests/configs/results and required Sneaking catalogue initialization. Followed `code-review`, phase 5, `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, domain vocabulary discovery, and repository testing guidance. Standards and specification reviewers worked separately and serially. Unrelated Research, Deathnote, Gambit, and other feature edits were excluded from Hole scope-expansion findings.

#### Standards

- **H8 — P2: Permanent pipeline tests assert wiring and incidental object replacement.** `tests/domains/hole/part1-pipeline.test.ts:85-100,117-119` assert method call counts and spy invocation order. Lines `101-110` freeze intermediate preparation state. Lines `251-263` require new array/object identities after repeated imports. These assertions reject equivalent implementations that retain the same public results, including combining preparation methods or changing independent late-step order. The assignment forbids permanent wiring/incidental-behavior tests; `docs/TESTING_IMPLEMENTATION.md:41-43` requires typed public results rather than incidental representation. Remove the observation-only spy/order/intermediate-state assertions and incidental identity checks. Keep paired numerical growth, schematic, Gambit, and Tome comparisons, character identity binding, and repeated-result/idempotence checks. The plan's ordering requirement does not exempt test design from this policy.
- **H9 — P3: Tests freeze explanation wording rather than verify effects.** `tests/domains/hole/studies.test.ts:148-152,159-160` require exact prose fragments in Study notes. These checks can fail after a correct wording improvement without any effect or scope change. Remove those prose-substring assertions; keep typed effect keys/scope and extracted numeric/discrete-rule comparisons. The same incidental-copy issue appears in acquisition/reset text assertions at `opal-ledger.test.ts:9-15,41,98-103` and Lantern explanation arrays at `lanterns.test.ts:64-69`. Those literal copies do not verify game semantics, as H12 demonstrates. This is a test-policy finding, not a request to remove the production explanations.

No additional independently actionable abstraction smell, impossible required-domain guard, or feature overengineering was confirmed. The five-role, all-character, 18-Study contracts serve approved outcomes. Focused named-input availability checks establish contracts, not additional captured game branches.

#### Specification

- **H10 — P2: Missing Hole Majik state still produces complete growth rates.** `hole.tsx:1330-1340` checks Village Majik (`Holes[5]`) but omits Hole Majik (`Holes[4]`). Parsing defaults absent Hole Majik levels to zero at `1351-1354`; monument rewards consume Monumental Vibes at `1427-1434`; villager growth consumes those rewards at `1211-1212`. An unknown monument modifier therefore becomes zero inside a numeric, supposedly complete rate. The plan's missing-input rule (`hole-part-1-backend-plan.md:66`) requires unavailable dependent values with reasons. Track this required save input and mark dependent rates/contributors unavailable. Preserve independent progression, Studies, and ledger information. This is a code-proven missing-input branch, not an observed game failure.
- **H11 — P2: Study rates omit required Legend Talent input availability.** `hole.tsx:1343-1347` omits `Spelunk` from required Study inputs. Study progress consumes collectible 16 at `1125,1141-1142`. `updateHole` applies Legend Talent 29 to every collectible at `1498-1502`; `jar.tsx:12-13` includes that multiplier. `legendTalents.tsx:80-82` returns on absent save input without resetting existing levels. Fresh instances can use an unverified zero talent bonus; reused instances can retain prior talent state. Target and eligible neighbors still receive numeric rates without a reason. Track the required Legend Talent save input and mark dependent Study rates and collectible contributions unavailable. Keep independent effects and inactive Study rates. This violates the same plan rule and remains a code-proven branch, not a captured runtime failure.
- **H12 — P2: Bell Opal acquisition names the wrong action.** `hole.tsx:1286` says “Received an Opal from a Bell ring.” The source-backed Bell findings distinguish Ring from Ping: Ring raises permanent bonuses; Ping gives an Opal (`hole-research-brief.md`, “Shallow Caverns: 1–5”, Bell entry). The plan requires each source row to identify its acquisition meaning (`hole-part-1-backend-plan.md:23`). Name Ping in this row and migrate the copied text assertion at `opal-ledger.test.ts:11`. Correct saved counts and ledger arithmetic do not validate this acquisition explanation.

#### H1–H7 assessment

- **H1 resolved:** `villager.tsx:223-317` owns role-specific preparation, availability precedence, contributor construction, and rate multiplication. Hole supplies shared and character-bound inputs.
- **H2 resolved, including required support:** `sneaking.tsx:404-423,464-467` initializes required Pristine Charm records before optional parsing, clears ownership before an absent-input return, and updates saved ownership. `hole.tsx:1545,1578` accesses required Candy Cache directly. Real unowned ownership still yields zero; missing Sneaking input still makes dependent growth unavailable.
- **H3 resolved in production:** `hole.tsx:1033-1042` prepares early shared Study/schematic inputs and cavern Opals. `1044-1065` prepares intrinsic unlocks and reset inspection through the late pipeline entry at `idleonData.tsx:407`. H8 concerns the verification design, not a confirmed production ordering failure.
- **H4 resolved:** `hole.tsx:1158-1176,1263-1264,1573` distinguishes unknown Superbit ownership from false. It preserves known target accrual and independent inactive Studies while making potential neighbor eligibility/rates unavailable.
- **H5 resolved:** `hole.tsx:1190-1200,1328` distinguishes missing Village Majik from zero allocation modifiers. Known counts remain; dependent percentages and contributor values become unavailable.
- **H6 resolved:** `hole.tsx:1214-1215,1328,1335` tracks actual card-save availability and distinguishes missing Gloomie input from verified zero.
- **H7 resolved:** `hole.tsx:379-387` names additive requirement-divisor contributions and explains their combination with collectibles. Refreshed extraction labels retain the observed totals 570%, 1870%, 3420%, and 972%. No complete cavern requirement calculator was added.

#### Evidence, scope, and decision boundary

- Retained six part 1 result artifacts contain 256 extraction entries and no recorded errors. The refreshed Study artifact contains 77 entries. Active-context records identify Player 3; retained growth, ledger, and Lantern values agree with the recorded revision evidence. The revision reports 17 suites / 444 tests, type checking, and scoped production lint passes. This review did not rerun them; tests remain outside lint coverage.

Fresh and repeated imports use one real save; they do not prove changed-account replacement. No genuine second-account or different-Statue game capture exists. Unobserved gameplay branches remain unobserved. Hand-composed effect and participation extraction expressions do not independently establish consumer semantics.
- Reviewed all five villagers, all 18 Studies, complete ledger/allocation/reset/Lantern contracts, early Study/schematic/Gambit consumers, late settled inputs, Tome's cavern-only migration, and removed-contract callers. No additional production ordering defect or scope expansion was confirmed. Jar, Exotic Market 51, and Sneaking initialization support remain necessary to approved outcomes. No forecast, simulation, recommendation, UI, store, endpoint, or game action was added.
- **Summary:** Standards: two findings, worst P2. Specification: three findings, worst P2. H1–H7 production fixes are resolved; H8–H12 await decisions. Code stayed read-only. Only this handoff changed. #### Standards

- **H13 — P2: Contributor tests still freeze display text and incidental order.** `tests/domains/hole/part1-parameters.test.ts:177-202,210-221` identify expected contributors by their display text and require the complete display-text array in a fixed order. Correct explanation changes or reordered unchanged contributions therefore fail the parameter tests. `docs/TESTING_IMPLEMENTATION.md:41-43` requires calculated public results, not incidental representation. Match expected values through stable owner identities and, where necessary, `sourceIds` or combination. Compare identity sets without ordering and retain every numerical comparison. This is a remaining H9-style policy issue outside the removed prose assertions.
- **H14 — P3: An availability test still requires an unchanged collection reference.** `tests/domains/hole/villager-growth.test.ts:218,229-230` checks other villagers' rate arrays with `toBe`. The required outcome is unchanged public rates, identities, and contributors, not unchanged array identity. Capture those values and compare them with `toEqual`. This violates the same result-focused test policy. H8 removed reference assertions from pipeline tests, but this separate occurrence remains.

These are documented test-policy findings, not heuristic smell claims. No additional independently actionable abstraction smell, impossible initialized-domain guard, or feature overengineering was confirmed. Named missing-save contracts serve an approved requirement; they do not justify guards for missing initialized objects.

#### Specification

- **H15 — P2: Missing collectible Legend Talent still permits complete villager growth.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:1338,1340-1351` records availability of the required collectible talent value but checks only the outer Legend Talent save for growth. Growth consumes collectible bonuses 4, 10, 12, 22, 29, and 35 at `1205-1206`. `updateHole` applies Legend Talent 29 to every collectible at `1510-1514`; `JarBonus.getBonus` multiplies that input at `jar.tsx:12-13`. A present but truncated talent array can retain the initial or prior talent level (`world-7/legendTalents.tsx:85-89`). Growth rates and the collectible contributor remain numeric despite the unknown multiplier. The plan's line 66 requires each dependent value to be unavailable with a reason. Reuse the existing named talent availability for growth and its collectible contributor. Extend the narrow omission contract to cover these affected character records while retaining independent information.
- **H16 — P2: A missing Compass save still exposes a numeric growth contributor.** `hole.tsx:1341` correctly makes the aggregate growth rate unavailable when Compass input is absent. However, `1588` still passes the Compass calculation, and `villager.tsx:250` publishes its numeric value without a reason. `compass.tsx:637-639` returns before updating absent save input, so that value can be initial zero or prior-account state. A contributor consumer cannot distinguish it from verified current input. Plan line 66 says missing inputs are not zero or complete totals. Carry the existing Compass availability into this contributor and report `null` with a reason. Preserve independent contributors; do not add a missing-domain guard or rewrite Compass.

H15 and H16 are code-proven missing-input defects, not newly observed game failures. They concern genuinely absent saved inputs, not impossible absence of initialized domains.

The pipeline artifact's value 259 is the extracted Tome score (`hole-part1-pipeline-data.json:154-156`), not the cavern-earned subtotal. The selected H17 revision corrects that narrative. This reporting discrepancy does not demonstrate incorrect production ledger arithmetic.
